{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_vrtx_01MRTs1q4cRgmVoKMxbo2rcq","type":"tool_result","content":"version = \"1.0\"\nreward_type = \"binary\"\n\n[task]\nname = \"scale-ai/space_wizards__space_station_14_36889_mm\"\n\n[metadata]\ntask_id = \"space_wizards__space_station_14_36889_mm\"\ntitle = \"Doors on the test bench misbehave\"\nrepository_name = \"space-wizards/space-station-14\"\nrepo_url = \"https://github.com/space-wizards/space-station-14\"\nissue_number = 36889\npr_number = 46007\nbase_commit = \"58dae1ce4a0806fa3b928834789b2b508b47b0f3\"\nfix_commit = \"763186613f196824f010bba663d353e4cce8f3ea\"\ntask_type = \"temporal_interaction\"\nlanguage = \"\"\nframework = \"Space Station 14\"\ncross_file = true\nvisual_essentiality = \"required\"\ndifficulty = \"hard\"\ndifficulty_explanation = \"\"\"\nThe clip is a firelock test bench recorded from the real client: six one-tile lanes, each firelock between a small room and a hazard (an exposed lattice open to space, dense gas, fire, fire behind a glass firelock, gas behind an edge firelock, and one firelock that starts open). The instruction names the doors only by type ('doors'), never the kind (firelock, glass firelock, edge firelock), the hazard, the lane or the direction, and it states no defect in words or how many there are. It names only the aspects that go wrong (the warning lights, the way a door moves, what ends up on each side of a shut door), adds one clip-only pointer ('some of them are already there in the first frame and never change', which covers the static lanes: the space lane's warning colour, the glass firelock with no warning, and the gas past the edge firelock's panel, without saying which lane, door or hazard), gives general expected rules (a shut door keeps each side apart and its warning says what kind of danger it holds back, the warning only shows while shut, a door shuts smoothly as a whole), and asks for every mismatch found in the clip to be fixed. Each Expected rule is enforced by f2p tests: 'keeps each side apart' -> ClosedFirelock_HoldsBackGasReleasedAfterClosing and EdgeFirelock_SealsAlongItsDoorEdge (the edge door's seal must sit on the edge its panel is drawn on, otherwise the hazard fills the door's own tile up to the room side of the panel, which the gas overlay shows); 'its warning says what kind of danger' -> WarningLights_SpaceBreachIsAPressureWarning and WarningLights_GlassFirelockShowsWarning; 'the warning only shows while shut' -> WarningLights_HideWhenDoorLeavesClosed; 'shuts smoothly, as a whole' -> ClosingAnimation_PlaysThroughBeforeTheDoorShuts ('smoothly': the panel plays through its closing frames instead of jumping shut in one frame; 'as a whole': the panel moves in step with its running lights instead of finishing while they are still sliding). Each of the six defects sits in a different place and most of them only reveal themselves in the clip:\n\n1. Warning lights ignore the door state (the upstream issue): the pressure/temperature layers are driven by a declarative GenericVisualizer that only reads the two warning booleans, so an opened or re-closing firelock keeps its brackets lit (covered by the 'warning only shows while shut' rule). The fix moves the layers into the client FirelockSystem's appearance handler (Closed/Welded/Denying only) and drops the visualizer.\n2. The closing animation is cut short: the frame timings of the standard firelock sprite's closing state (RSI meta.json) are ten times too short, so the panel jumps from open to shut in one frame and its green running lights then slide over an already-shut door (the opening in the same clip slides normally, and the opening state in the same meta.json is the reference). The prototype's animation times and the client door code are both correct, so only frame-stepping the clip points at it.\n3. The lattice/space lane shows the temperature warning instead of the pressure one: the firelock's temperature-difference threshold default was lowered so that space's ~2.7 K against a room at 293 K counts as a temperature hazard. The over-pressure lane next to it shows the pressure colour, the fire lanes the temperature colour.\n4. The glass firelock shows no warning at all while holding back fire (it still glows): its prototype overrides the sprite layer list and leaves out the two warning layers, so the old visualizer only reserves blank layers. Needs prototype-inheritance knowledge.\n5. The edge firelock seals the wrong edge of its tile: its airBlockedDirection was flipped, so gas fills the door's own tile past the hazard strip (its fixture and sprite sit on the other edge). Only the gas overlay in the clip shows it.\n6. A firelock shut during the round does not seal: AirtightSystem.SetAirblocked only re-validates the atmos tile when a door opens (a two-line conditional with no comment), so a door that closes later never blocks air and the gas released behind it flows straight through. Doors that were shut by monstermos or at map load are unaffected, which is why the other lanes hold.\n\nWithout the clip the instruction does not say which kind of firelock, which hazard, which animation or which direction is wrong; with it, every defect except the open door's lights has a correct counterpart in another lane, and that one is covered by an expected rule. The fix touches five files: client visuals, shared component data, server atmos, three prototypes in one YAML file, and one sprite's frame metadata.\n\"\"\"\nfiles_edited = [\n small walled test bench for doors: a one-tile-wide lane on its own grid, with a lower cell (y = 0..1),\n+/// the door tile (y = 2) and an upper cell (y = 3..4). The upper cell can instead be left open to space.\n+/// Everything here goes through public game systems only: spawn, open/close, read and change tile air, and\n+/// read what the client draws.\n+/// </summary>\n+internal sealed class FirelockLane\n+{\n+    public static readonly Vector2i DoorTile = new(0, 2);\n+    public static readonly Vector2i[] Lower = { new(0, 0), new(0, 1) };\n+    public static readonly Vector2i[] Upper = { new(0, 3), new(0, 4) };\n+\n+    public EntityUid Grid;\n+    public EntityUid Door;\n+\n+    private readonly GameTest _t;\n+\n+    private FirelockLane(GameTest t)\n+    {\n+        _t = t;\n+    }\n+\n+    public static async Task<FirelockLane> Build(GameTest t, string doorProto, bool upperIsSpace = false)\n+    {\n+        var lane = new FirelockLane(t);\n+\n+        await t.Server.WaitPost(() =>\n+        {\n+            var mapSys = t.Server.System<SharedMapSystem>();\n+            var tileDefs = t.Server.ResolveDependency<ITileDefinitionManager>();\n+            var floor = tileDefs[\"FloorSteel\"].TileId;\n+            var plating = tileDefs[\"Plating\"].TileId;\n+\n+            mapSys.CreateMap(out var mapId);\n+            var grid = mapSys.CreateGridEntity(mapId);\n+            lane.Grid = grid.Owner;\n+\n+            var walls = new List<Vector2i>();\n+            for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x++)\n+            {\n+                for (var y = -1; y <= 5; y++)\n+                {\n+                    // Open to space: no floor above the door, and no wall closing it off at the top.\n+                    if (upperIsSpace && x == 0 && y >= 3)\n+                        continue;\n+\n+                    var isWall = x != 0 || y == -1 || y == 5;\n+                    mapSys.SetTile(grid.Owner, grid.Comp, new Vector2i(x, y), new Tile(isWall ? plating : floor));\n+                    if (isWall)\n+                        walls.Add(new Vector2i(x, y));\n+                }\n+            }\n+\n+            t.SEntMan.EnsureComponent<GridAtmosphereComponent>(grid.Owner);\n+            t.SEntMan.EnsureComponent<GasTileOverlayComponent>(grid.Owner);\n+\n+            foreach (var w in walls)\n+                t.SEntMan.SpawnEntity(\"WallSolid\", new EntityCoordinates(grid.Owner, w.X + 0.5f, w.Y + 0.5f));\n+\n+            lane.Door = t.SEntMan.SpawnEntity(doorProto, new EntityCoordinates(grid.Owner, DoorTile.X + 0.5f, DoorTile.Y + 0.5f));\n+        });\n+\n+        await t.RunTicksSync(5);\n+        return lane;\n+    }\n+\n+    public DoorState ServerState => _t.SEntMan.GetComponent<DoorComponent>(Door).State;\n+\n+    public async Task WaitForState(DoorState state)\n+    {\n+        await PoolManager.WaitUntil(_t.Server, () => ServerState == state);\n+        Assert.That(ServerState, Is.EqualTo(state));\n+    }\n+\n+    /// <summary>\n+    /// Shuts the door and waits until it is fully closed.\n+    /// </summary>\n+    /// <param name=\"asIfMappedShut\">\n+    /// Also have atmos re-read the door tile once, the way a door that was already shut when the map loaded is\n+    /// seen. Tests that are not about how a door seals use this, so they only depend on what they check.\n+    /// </param>\n+    public async Task Close(bool asIfMappedShut)\n+    {\n+        var doors = _t.Server.System<SharedDoorSystem>();\n+        await _t.Server.WaitPost(() => doors.TryClose(Door));\n+        await WaitForState(DoorState.Closed);\n+\n+        if (asIfMappedShut)\n+            await _t.Server.WaitPost(() => _t.Server.System<AtmosphereSystem>().InvalidateTile(Grid, DoorTile));\n+\n+        await _t.RunTicksSync(5);\n+    }\n+\n+    /// <summary>\n+    /// Gives every tile on the lane the normal round-start air.\n+    /// </summary>\n+    public async Task FillAir()\n+    {\n+        await _t.Server.WaitPost(() =>\n+        {\n+            var atmos = _t.Server.System<AtmosphereSystem>();\n+            atmos.RebuildGridAtmosphere((Grid,\n+                _t.SEntMan.GetComponent<GridAtmosphereComponent>(Grid),\n+                _t.SEntMan.GetComponent<MapGridComponent>(Grid)));\n+        });\n+        await _t.RunTicksSync(2);\n+    }\n+\n+    public async Task ChangeAir(IEnumerable<Vector2i> tiles, Action<GasMixture> change)\n+    {\n+        await _t.Server.WaitPost(() =>\n+        {\n+            var atmos = _t.Server.System<AtmosphereSystem>();\n+            foreach (var tile in tiles)\n+            {\n+                var mix = atmos.GetTileMixture((Grid, null, null), null, tile, excite: true);\n+                Assert.That(mix, Is.Not.Null, $\"Tile {tile} has no air to change.\");\n+                change(mix!);\n+            }\n+        });\n+    }\n+\n+    public async Task<float> Moles(Gas gas, params Vector2i[] tiles)\n+    {\n+        var total = 0f;\n+        await _t.Server.WaitPost(() =>\n+        {\n+            var atmos = _t.Server.System<AtmosphereSystem>();\n+            foreach (var tile in tiles)\n+                total += atmos.GetTileMixture((Grid, null, null), null, tile)?.GetMoles(gas) ?? 0f;\n+        });\n+        return total;\n+    }\n+\n+    /// <summary>\n+    /// What the client draws for a keyed sprite layer of the door: whether the layer is shown, and whether it\n+    /// actually has a sprite state to draw (a blank placeholder layer draws nothing).\n+    /// </summary>\n+    public async Task<(bool Shown, bool Drawable)> ClientLayer(Enum key)\n+    {\n+        var shown = false;\n+        var drawable = false;\n+        var clientUid = _t.ToClientUid(Door);\n+\n+        await _t.Client.WaitPost(() =>\n+        {\n+            var spriteSys = _t.Client.System<SpriteSystem>();\n+            var sprite = _t.CEntMan.GetComponent<SpriteComponent>(clientUid);\n+            if (!spriteSys.LayerMapTryGet((clientUid, sprite), key, out var index, false)\n+                || !spriteSys.TryGetLayer((clientUid, sprite), index, out var layer, false))\n+                return;\n+\n+            shown = layer.Visible;\n+            drawable = layer.ActualRsi is { } rsi && rsi.TryGetState(layer.State, out _);\n+        });\n+\n+        return (shown, drawable);\n+    }\n+\n+    public async Task<bool> WarningShown(Enum key)\n+    {\n+        var (shown, drawable) = await ClientLayer(key);\n+        return shown && drawable;\n+    }\n+}\ndiff --git a/Content.IntegrationTests/Tests/Doors/FirelockSealTest.cs b/Content.IntegrationTests/Tests/Doors/FirelockSealTest.cs\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 00000000..b961ddb8\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/Content.IntegrationTests/Tests/Doors/FirelockSealTest.cs\n@@ -0,0 +1,75 @@\n+#nullable enable\n+using Content.IntegrationTests.Fixtures;\n+using Content.Shared.Atmos;\n+using Content.Shared.Doors.Components;\n+using Robust.Shared.GameObjects;\n+\n+namespace Content.IntegrationTests.Tests.Doors;\n+\n+/// <summary>\n+/// What a shut firelock keeps out.      var behind = await lane.Moles(Gas.Plasma, FirelockLane.Upper);\n+        var through = await lane.Moles(Gas.Plasma, FirelockLane.Lower);\n+        Assert.Multiple(() =>\n+        {\n+ Assert.That(held, Is.GreaterThan(Released), \"The released gas should still be below the door panel.\");\n+            Assert.That(onDoorTile, Is.LessThan(Trace),\n+                $\"{onDoorTile:F1} mol of gas reached the edge firelock's own tile from beyond its door panel.\");\n+            Assert.That(past, Is.LessThan(Trace),\n+                $\"{past:F1} mol of gas got past the edge
